The following events are triggered by an advance, debate, or stall checkpoint in the [law enactment process](Laws.md).

## Generic law enactment events

These laws can be triggered by any country when enacting the given law(s). They have a few things in common:

- They all have a cooldown of 5 years, unless no other events are available for a given checkpoint
- They do not trigger if the relevant interest groups and characters do not exist or do not satisfy the given conditions
- The event itself is canceled if the law enactment is canceled
- Any enactment modifier effect lasts until the law is enacted or canceled

| Event | Enacting laws | Event type | Other conditions | Relevant IGs and characters | Options |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

The body of [target leader] has been found amongst the rubble. "War, class war, and you were the first to wage it under the cover of the powerful institutions you call order, in the darkness of your laws. There will have to be bloodshed; we will not dodge; there will have to be murder: we will kill, because it is necessary; there will have to be destruction; we will destroy to rid the world of your tyrannical institutions." | Anarchy | Stall | Does not have Anarchist Terrorism Anarchist Terrorism Enactment modifier −10% Enactment Success Chance | Target : Random non-marginalized IG with Reactionary, Paternalistic, Magnatial, Carlist Carlist or Carlist (modern) , or Moderantist and available leader | No more than these reactionaries deserve. My brother and I stood there, quiet as a tomb, you see, I held my breath as much as I could, but it was too dark, so I figured they had already left. | Second Class Citizens citizenship_laws.3 [ future_discriminated_pop culture name ] people have spoken out against the passing of [current law] legislation, arguing that they deserve the same rights as any other citizens. "What has possessed the leaders of this nation to think of us as enemies? Have we not toiled together, suffered together, eaten at the same tables and lived as brothers and sisters up until now? Our children will remember a country their parents helped build, a country that treated them fairly, as a country that turned its back on them when overcome with bigotry and malice. | [ future_accepted_pop culture name ] Customers Not Wanted citizenship_laws.6 Some $shopkeepers$ have expressed opposition to the equal treatment of [ future_accepted_pop culture name ] people under the [current law] law, announcing that they will refuse them service. | Filling the Ranks conscription_laws.1 Members of the [ rural_folk_conscription interestgroup name ] have approached the government about the implementation of [current law]. They believe that more time is needed before we are ready to implement this law. ""Would you put a price on the freedom that we all enjoy? No! But you do put a price on the crops those workers grow. Our freedom and national security is not up for debate, nor can you put a price on it. We must ensure our nation’s survival, and every man must be prepared to do what is necessary to obtain that goal." | Mass Conscription Peasant Levies | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_professional_army OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription }NOT = { has_modifier = conscription_starting } | ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { save_scope_as = rural_folk_conscription } | Push forward on passing the law. add_modifier = { name = keep_marching_on days = normal_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_starting multiplier = 2 } Lets take our time with this. add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_starting } |
| Professional Troops conscription_laws.2 Many of our professional soldiers have brought up the concerns that mixing conscripts with regular infantry during war will greatly affect the quality and competence of our troops. "You expect me to go fight and put my life on the line for this country, and you want me to fight along with some farmer or factory worker that has never so much as held a rifle before? How can we expect our forces to work in unison together when only part of them knows what they are doing?" | Mass Conscription Peasant Levies | Debate | has_law = law_type:law_professional_army OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription }NOT = { has_modifier = conscription_debate } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_conscription } | We need all the soldiers we can get. scope:armed_forces_conscription = { add_modifier = { name = more_soldiers days = normal_modifier_time } }random_list = { 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 3 } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 2 } } } We should think this through. add_modifier = { name = listen_to_the_troops days = normal_modifier_time }add_loyalists = { pop_type = soldiers value = 0.1 }add_loyalists = { pop_type = officers value = 0.1 }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 5 } } 4 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 1 } } } |
| Unprepared for Conscription conscription_laws.3 [ anti_conscription_advocate character name ] the leader of the [ anti_conscription_ig interestgroup name ] has given a rousing speech against the dangers of implementing [current law] so rapidly. "We cannot just ignore the fact that we are not ready for this. What good is it if the country collapses under us? If we send out the mobilization order when we are unprepared, not only will we need to pay our new soldiers, but we will also have less workers in the factories and farmers in the fields. Which means even less production, which leads to less profit, which finally leads us to less taxes and national revenue. | The National Unions corporate_state.1 Segments of the Trade Unions have voiced their support for the official recognition of the role of labor within our state. They serve as a valuable counterbalance against the communist opposition. "Today the bourgeoisie itself shows a sympathy and an inclination, which are by no means illogical, towards the unionisation of the working class. Indeed, the more intelligent sections of the bourgeoisie would readily accept a reform of the state and representative apparatus in order to give a larger place to the 'apolitical' unions and even to their claims to exercise control over the system of production. The bourgeoisie feels that, as long as the proletariat's action can be limited to the immediate economic demands that are raised trade by trade, it helps to safeguard the status-quo and to avoid the formation of the perilous 'political' consciousness..." | Corporate State Corporatized Unions Social Monarchy | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { leader = { NOR = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical } } ig_counts_as_marginal = no ig_approval > 0 } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } if = { limit = { any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } } random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = relevant_movement } } | Their support is welcomed. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_national_unions } Use them to recruit for our national labor organization. custom_tooltip = { text = corporatist_leaders_boost_tt scope:trade_unions_ig = { set_variable = { name = corporatist_leaders_boost days = very_long_modifier_time } } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_national_unions multiplier = 0.5 } An excellent opportunity to discredit our opponents. trigger = { any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state }random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_opposition_discredited days = short_modifier_time } } |
| Socialization corporate_state.2 Elements in our government have begun to debate the nationalization of key industries under the structure of the proposed [current law]. Many private entrepreneurs have argued against the proposal. The owner of the factory was a small man, given inflated stature by heeled shoes, a fleshy face, and an impressive hat. A clear benefit of his fortunate position, and a fact that he was deeply conscious of. "I was a cobbler from thirteen," he began, gesturing wildly towards the window to the shop floor. "And my mentor was a great man. I inherited the store from him at twenty, and purchased this factory nine years later. This town would be nothing without me. Do you understand that? I won't hear this. | Irreconcilable Differences corporate_state.3 News of brutal labor abuses in the [ relevant_building building name ] of [ relevant_state state name ] have spread across the nation. Dissidents have pointed to this as proof that the government's proposed [current law] cannot be trusted to protect workers' rights. "Every day, we hear stories about our class brothers losing their lives, whether in whole or in part, to the depredations of their employers. To this observation, the employers reply that they, too, are burdened, and that they provide for us as much as we provide for them. Why, then, do we never hear of the owners losing their fingers, or their eyesight? Why do they die at sixty-five, whilst we die at thirty? It has become routine that our corpses are carted out of their factories, whilst the worst thing they may suffer is becoming one of us!" | Corporate State Social Monarchy | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } } save_scope_as = relevant_state random_scope_pop = { limit = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } save_scope_as = relevant_pop workplace = { save_scope_as = relevant_building } } } | Announce a legal action against the owners. ig:ig_industrialists ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_legal_prosecution days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses } Suppress this story before it can spread further. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses_suppressed } What do we care for some [ relevant_pop pop culture name ] wastrels? trigger = { scope:relevant_pop = { pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4 } }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = scope:relevant_pop.culture }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses multiplier = 0.5 } |
| The Residual Category corporate_state.4 Through visibly favoring sectors of employment deemed productive, the government has successfully built support for the establishment of a [current law]. Their opponents have been decried as unproductive and parasitic. "When one analyzes the sum of production in the capitalist economy, one comes to the conclusion that there are two primary categories into which the population may be subdivided. The productive category - those who produce material alterations of nature - and the residual category, those who do not. The former produces value, the latter voraciously consumes it. This latter category includes, in capitalist society, the capitalists themselves, bureaucrats of all sorts, security forces, clerks, professionals, and "service workers". The former category justifies its existence by creating the conditions to reproduce itself - but how does the latter?" | Corporate State Social Monarchy | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy }NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_multicultural } | random_primary_culture = { save_scope_as = primary_culture_scope } | Quality work is a [ primary_culture_scope culture name ] tradition. add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_chauvinism days = normal_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_productive_labor } For an honest regime of productive classes! add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_class_unity days = normal_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_productive_labor } |

A wall of black ink, lists of every officially-recognized [country-adj] trade union and employer's confederations. | Unqualified to Lead corporate_state.6 The proposal to establish a [current law] has met pushback from the [ relevant_ig interestgroup name ], who claim that the proposal will permit unqualified workers too much control over their enterprises. "For as long as I remember, we have adhered to the same contract. We provide direction to those who work for us, and the quality of this direction is dependent upon my discretion. I have the fortune of working in the field of furniture-making for forty years... these people you wish to match my authority, how many of them are literate? How many possess a degree?" The functionary behind the desk nodded along as the proprietor spoke, brow furrowed. This man felt threatened, he reminded himself, and he had the best of intentions. Whether that was true or not was irrelevant - it was the mantra itself that centered him. | The Old Authority distribution_of_power_laws.9 The [current law] reform threatens the power held by many leading figures in [country], some of whom are using the political connections they have to obstruct its passing. "Between you and me, there are far too many high-minded idealists involved in this. They find something noble in giving up their own influence, spreading it to the masses like alms to the poor. Perhaps they think it will save their souls, or their legacies. I am more practically inclined. I'm concerned with the immediate future of myself and my friends. I can count you among those. Can't I?" | Census Suffrage Universal Suffrage | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage }NOR = { has_law = law_type:law_census_voting has_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage has_law = law_type:law_anarchy }NOT = { has_modifier = fresh_start }NOT = { has_modifier = collaboration_with_old_leadership } |  | They must be ousted from their positions if they try to stop the new law! trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy } add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = capitalists } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_landed_voting } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = officers } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = clergymen } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = capitalists } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals strata = upper } } Perhaps we can collaborate with them in the creation of the new system. add_enactment_modifier = { name = collaboration_with_old_leadership } |
| Sins of the Past distribution_of_power_laws.10 Proponents of the [current law] reform have called attention to past abuses of power by the current regime as a reason for why this change is urgently needed. "Well, I barely remember it myself. It was a different time. Things like that simply happened, and I never expected to be interrogated about them. That's not to say it went unquestioned, but there are always complainers. You couldn't listen to them if you wanted to get things done. Of course, this was all with the [title]'s approval. Not officially, but I think you should know. That stays between us." | Anarchy Census Suffrage Landed Voting Universal Suffrage Wealth Voting | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_single_party_state }NOT = { has_modifier = admitted_abuses_of_power }NOT = { has_modifier = downplayed_abuses_of_power } |  | Power corrupts, which is why the powerful must be held to account by the voters. add_enactment_modifier = { name = admitted_abuses_of_power }every_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes } leader = { add_modifier = { name = corrupted_by_power days = normal_modifier_time } } } The past is in the past. These issues have nothing to do with the reform. add_enactment_modifier = { name = downplayed_abuses_of_power } |
| Democracy Without the People distribution_of_power_laws.11 Proponents of Universal Suffrage have argued that the ongoing reform to [current law] is an insufficient compromise, excluding many people from their right to participate in democracy. "'[Current law]'. Just the sound of it! As if that would mean we cast of the yoke of oppression, rather than shifting its burden. How are we supposed to rally in solidarity behind this reform when it still leaves so many without access to the franchise? As usual, they are expected to be glad that anything is happening at all. To have no expectations at all for their own lives. That is no way to treat a human being." | Census Suffrage Landed Voting Wealth Voting | Debate | has_technology_researched = egalitarianism OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_single_party_state }any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } NOT = { has_modifier = fear_of_future_reforms } }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage value > neutral } ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_modifier = current_election_focus } | law_type:law_universal_suffrage = { save_scope_as = universal_suffrage_type } | We should focus on our current reform, which is a clear improvement. add_enactment_modifier = { name = current_election_focus }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage value > neutral } } add_modifier = { name = democratic_compromise days = normal_modifier_time } } [Current law] is a stepping stone on the road to Universal Suffrage! random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = fear_of_future_reforms days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| The Paperwork of Elections distribution_of_power_laws.12 Implementing a proper voting system for the upcoming [current law] elections has put a strain on the [country-adj] government's administrative capacities. "Once we have the whole infrastructure in place, it will be much easier. We need to prove that we can do this. People need to be able to trust that their votes are properly collected and counted, or they will quickly dismiss the elections as a sham. The people are watching. Other nations are watching. We can't afford to take shortcuts." | Census Suffrage Landed Voting Universal Suffrage Wealth Voting | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_single_party_state }NOT = { has_modifier = administrative_assistance_for_elections }NOT = { has_modifier = setting_up_election_system } |  | We will use resources from other administrative branches, if necessary. add_enactment_modifier = { name = administrative_assistance_for_elections } We will simply have to take our time. add_enactment_modifier = { name = setting_up_election_system } |
| Growth Exceeds Projections economy_laws.1 The central economic office has declared that our efforts to bring economic matters under government control has resulted in surprisingly positive growth. The man nervously adjusted his glasses. "Frankly, I'm amazed," he admitted. "I've run the numbers twice and had three clerks down in the office check my work, and wouldn't you know it? We're ahead of schedule and above our projected rates! Why, I think I might celebrate!" He reached for his bowtie, and after a moment's fumbling, took it off. "Look out, world, I'm cutting loose tonight!" he gleefully cried. | Command Economy | Advance |  |  | Excellent! Proceed with the legislation. trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 Excellent! Let's support the clerks who made this possible. add_modifier = { name = projected_growth_models_bureau days = short_modifier_time } |
| Party Disputes economy_laws.2 A faction of the government is objecting to the pace and approach to our changing economic policy, and wishes to discuss and revise our current agenda. "While I know some accuse me of oppositionist tendencies, I can assure everyone gathered here that I am a loyal man of the party. All I am saying, friends, is that we need to consider whether our policy has matured enough to discard the outmoded social-economic system entirely." | Command Economy | Debate | NOT = { has_variable = command_economy_law_debate_timer } | set_variable = { name = command_economy_law_debate_timer days = 730 # two years } | Let party members debate the issue. add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_debate } Push our stance at party conferences. random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_debate_success } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_debate_fail } } } Have a little chat with our opponents. trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_secret_police }random_list = { 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_debate_success } } 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_debate_fail } } } |
| Dizzy With Success economy_laws.3 Our efforts to push our reform of the economy through has caused shortages and mismanagement in [ state_run_state state name ]. "It is a fact that we have overfulfilled our planned reform of the economy, and our friends among the [ command_econ_proponent_ig interestgroup name ] have become dizzy with success and for the moment have lost clearness of mind and sobriety of vision." | Command Economy | Stall | NOT = { has_variable = command_economy_law_stall_timer } | set_variable = { name = command_economy_law_stall_timer days = 730 # two years } random_scope_state = { save_scope_as = state_run_state } random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes } save_scope_as = command_econ_proponent_ig } | We must own our mistakes. trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 scope:command_econ_proponent_ig = { add_modifier = { name = ig_dizzy_with_success days = normal_modifier_time } } We have no failures, only successes! scope:state_run_state = { add_modifier = { name = state_dizzy_with_success days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = command_economy_stall } |
| Indefensible Extortion economy_laws.4 An orator from the [ pro_free_trade_ig interestgroup name ] has gone on a speaking tour across the nation, denouncing [standing law] and praising the value of Free Trade. "When we consider that the theory of our great system guarantees to everyone the full enjoyment of all the fruits of their industry and enterprise, it is clear that anything other than the free flow of goods is an indefensible extortion and culpable betrayal of our values." | Free Trade | Advance | any_interest_group = { is_in_government = yes law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_law = { limit = { type = law_type:law_mercantilism # To get the trade law category } currently_active_law_in_group = { save_scope_as = standing_law_scope } } random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = pro_free_trade_ig } | We work towards a common goal! add_enactment_modifier = { name = freeing_the_markets } The [ pro_free_trade_ig interestgroup name ] has a truly remarkable speaker among them. scope:pro_free_trade_ig = { add_modifier = { name = free_market_ideologue days = short_modifier_time } } |

| Academic Assistance education_laws.1 The teachers of our higher institutions of learning have offered to advise the government in the implementation of the new [current law]. "There are certainly differences between teaching children the fundamentals and the more advanced education offered at our universities. Nevertheless, as architects of the upper floors of this towering structure of knowledge, we believe the least we can do is provide some insight into how the foundation should be built." | Private Schools Public Schools Religious Schools | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }any_scope_state = { has_building = building_university }NOT = { has_modifier = assisting_professors }NOT = { has_modifier = university_support } |  | We'll be happy to have their help! add_enactment_modifier = { name = assisting_professors } Their research work is more important. add_enactment_modifier = { name = university_support } They could be of help in finalizing the bill. trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 |
| Discipline and Punish education_laws.2 The role of discipline in the new [current law] has been raised by the Armed Forces as an area of improvement in the plans for this new education system. "This is not merely about keeping an orderly classroom so that knowledge can be imparted; it is about imparting character. After all, we have been given an enormous responsibility by the parents of these children, who do all they can to raise them as upstanding citizens. Too often, we find boys joining the military service whose character has already been almost irrevocably ruined by an unruly youth and a lack of respect for authority. It needn't be so!" | Private Schools Public Schools Religious Schools | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }NOT = { has_modifier = curriculum_focus }NOT = { has_modifier = discipline_focus }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_prussian_education } } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_ig } | We should focus on the curriculum. add_enactment_modifier = { name = curriculum_focus }ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = undisciplined_children days = normal_modifier_time } } Working this into the school system is paramount. add_enactment_modifier = { name = discipline_focus }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_prussian_education sponsor = scope:armed_forces_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| Curriculum Disagreements education_laws.3 Disagreements within the government about what should be taught in the new [current law] have brought implementation of the reform to a standstill. "These classes may have been well suited for the tutoring of noblemen two centuries ago, but believing they would serve a broader range of students well in this day and age is outright lunacy!" "And you mean to say your 'modern' alternatives would serve them better? | Overzealous Censorship free_speech_laws.3 The [current law] law is having trouble gaining popular support as references to it are being suppressed by the current censorship apparatus. "Anything regarding censorship is immediately censored, so how on earth are we meant to have an open discussion about it?" "First time, eh? They're only as smart as what they understand. If they're none too literate, you can slip a few metaphors and similes past." "What in blazes is a simile? | Mistreatment of Serfs labor_rights.1 [ serf_mistreaters interestgroup name ] in [ serf_state state name ] have become notorious for brutalizing their serfs. "People have been going missing on the estate on the hill." "There's a dungeon below the basements, my cousin swears upon it! They torture us and drink our blood!" | Collectivized Agriculture Commercialized Agriculture Homesteading Peasant Proprietorship Tenant Farmers | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_serfdom OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture }any_scope_state = { any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants } }NOT = { has_modifier = serf_mistreaters_demonized_enactment } | random_scope_state = { limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants } } save_scope_as = serf_state } ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = serf_mistreaters } | These indignities must end. add_enactment_modifier = { name = serf_mistreaters_demonized_enactment multiplier = 2 } We will punish the aristocrats with the full force of the law. scope:serf_mistreaters = { add_modifier = { name = serf_mistreaters_demonized days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = serf_mistreaters_demonized_enactment } |
| Emancipators labor_rights.2 A group of influential [ anti_serfdom_ig interestgroup name ] led by [ anti_serfdom_advocate character name ] are petitioning for the rapid abolition of serfdom. "I could wax lyrical about the moral virtues of emancipation all day, and with good reason. But the simple truth of the matter is the serfdom is inefficient. It is economically ruinous, an obstacle to the rightful progression of our nation both in industry and society. Serfs who work the fields would be better put to use in factories, where they can be gainfully employed and properly industrious." | Collectivized Agriculture Commercialized Agriculture Homesteading Peasant Proprietorship Tenant Farmers | Debate | has_law = law_type:law_serfdom OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_serfdom value < neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = anti_serfdom_debate } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_serfdom value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_serfdom_ig leader = { save_scope_as = anti_serfdom_advocate } } | They make an excellent case. add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = 1 } Such an inspiring speaker! scope:anti_serfdom_advocate = { add_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_popularity days = normal_modifier_time } }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = 3 } } 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = -1 } } } |
| Purchasing Emancipation labor_rights.3 The [ serf_owners interestgroup name ] refuse to give up their serfs without adequate compensation. Their significant political influence makes this a difficult demand to refuse. "We have relied on serf labor for generations. You cannot simply ask us release them without compensation! How are we to keep our estate profitable? How are we to maintain the standards of living we are accustomed to? Our relative poverty would make us the laughing stock of the [country-adj] aristocracy." | Commercialized Agriculture Homesteading Peasant Proprietorship Tenant Farmers | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_serfdom OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship }ig:ig_landowners ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }currently_enacting_law = { NOR = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_redemption_payments has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_individual_redemption_payments } }NAND = { # Japan should have trouble getting rid of Serfdom before Restoration is complete c:JAP ?= this NOT = { has_variable = japan_restoration_complete } } | ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = serf_owners } | The state will make up the cost. currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_redemption_payments sponsor = scope:serf_owners cooldown = 120 timeout = 600 } }scope:serf_owners ?= { improve_law_stance = yes } Serfs must purchase their own emancipation. currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_individual_redemption_payments sponsor = scope:serf_owners cooldown = 120 timeout = 600 } }scope:serf_owners ?= { improve_law_stance = yes } You cannot own a human being. trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = no_buying_serfs }add_radicals = { value = large_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } |
| Direct Action labor_rights.4 Workers organized by the [ unions_scope interestgroup name ] are staging short strikes and work-to-rule periods in an effort to pressure the government to pass the [current law] law. Though their methods seem effective, they are causing major damage to our industrial economy. "We have the right to be safe at work! Nobody should have to clock in wondering whether today will be the day they lose an arm or an eye. Until our safety is guaranteed, we'll not work another day." | Regulatory Bodies Workers' Protections | Advance | OR = { AND = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies has_law = law_type:law_no_workers_rights } is_enacting_law = law_type:law_worker_protections }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_modifier = enactment_strikes_modifier }NOT = { has_modifier = enactment_strikes_deterred_modifier } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_scope } | Let them strike! add_enactment_modifier = { name = enactment_strikes_modifier } Offer assurances and try to de-escalate. add_enactment_modifier = { name = enactment_strikes_deterred_modifier } |
| Worker Safety Negotiations labor_rights.5 [ capitalists_scope interestgroup name ] have agreed to state-mediated negotiations with the [ unions_scope interestgroup name ] over the implementation of [current law]. "Our profit margins are going to suffer, you must understand this. That won't be good for anybody, including you. Why we may even have to let some of you go, and how will you feed your families then?" "Do you know what's really bad for business? Angry workers. Injured workers. Dead workers." | Regulatory Bodies Workers' Protections | Debate | OR = { AND = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies has_law = law_type:law_no_workers_rights } is_enacting_law = law_type:law_worker_protections }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_modifier = worker_protection_negotiations } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_scope } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = capitalists_scope } | Support the [ unions_scope interestgroup name ]. add_enactment_modifier = { name = worker_protection_negotiations }add_loyalists = { strata = lower value = 0.05 } Make concessions to the [ capitalists_scope interestgroup name ]. add_enactment_modifier = { name = worker_protection_negotiations multiplier = -1 }add_loyalists = { strata = upper value = 0.1 } |
| Strikebreakers labor_rights.6 The [ capitalists_scope interestgroup name ] are paying a group of armed thugs calling themselves a private investigation agency to break up [ unions_scope interestgroup name ] agitation for [current law]. "We're just making sure that everyone is following the law here. We don't want one of those angry workers firing a shot or starting a riot. We're just here to keep the peace, for the right price. And maybe in the process some legs get broken, some skulls get cracked. That's just the cost of public safety." | Regulatory Bodies Workers' Protections | Stall | OR = { AND = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies has_law = law_type:law_no_workers_rights } is_enacting_law = law_type:law_worker_protections }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_modifier = strikbreakers_enactment } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_scope } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = capitalists_scope } | Arrest them for assault. scope:capitalists_scope = { add_modifier = { name = strikebreakers_called_off days = short_modifier_time multiplier = -1 } }scope:unions_scope = { add_modifier = { name = strikebreakers_called_off days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = strikbreakers_enactment } We cannot risk angering the [ capitalists_scope interestgroup name ] further. scope:capitalists_scope = { add_modifier = { name = strikebreakers_allowed days = short_modifier_time } }scope:unions_scope = { add_modifier = { name = strikebreakers_allowed days = short_modifier_time multiplier = -1 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = strikbreakers_enactment multiplier = -1 } |
| Union Free labour_associations_events.1 Threatened by the strengthening of workers' associations under [current law], the Industrialists have publicized numerous reports on the dangers of a unionized workforce. #bold Is Union Life For Me?#! • Dues deducted from your wages • No guarantees on pay, benefit, or work rules • No chance for individual negotiation #italic Your COMPANY provides your job and pays your wages - the UNION never has and never will!#! |  | Stall | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes OR = { AND = { c:BIC ?= this ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } AND = { NOT = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } } | if = { limit = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } | They must be terrified. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_anti_union_propaganda multiplier = 0.5 }scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_anti_union_propaganda_ig days = short_modifier_time } } Everyone has the right to pursue their interests. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_anti_union_propaganda } |
| Preemptive Strikes labour_associations_events.2 The news of the government's efforts to enshrine the [current law] has spread rapidly through working-class communities. The Industrialists have begun to complain of a corresponding uptick in illegal union activity. The moment the government announced its initiative, the posters started appearing. The vain efforts of the night-watchmen to catch the perpetrators bore no fruit, forcing the firm into damage-control. Every night, the walls were plastered - and in the morning, the company filled the bins with the last night's efforts. The very next week, the garbagemen announced their new union. | Right to Associate | Advance | OR = { AND = { c:BIC ?= this ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } AND = { NOT = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } } | if = { limit = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } | Order the courts to disregard these complaints. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_preempetive_strikes }scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_preempetive_strikes_ig days = short_modifier_time } } The present laws remain in effect. scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_slow_and_boring days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = machinists }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = laborers } |
| The Commission on Trade Unions labour_associations_events.3 Following petitions from labor groups, [legislature] has assembled a commission on [current law]. The commission has delivered two reports to the government - a majority report written by the [ opposed_ig interestgroup name ], and a minority report sympathetic to the [ unions_ig interestgroup name ]. "In fact, the law as it stood is one-sided and unjust. For while nearly every combination of workmen in furtherance of their objects was held to be a conspiracy in restraint of trade, and therefore criminally punishable, precisely similar acts committed by a master escaped the notice of the law, as not involving the offence of conspiracy. But, in fact, the employer of some thousands of workmen was, in himself, a combination." | Labor association | Debate | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes country_has_voting_franchise = yes any_interest_group = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_ig } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = opposed_ig } | Adopt the minority report. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_favorable_trade_union_report }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_favorable_report days = short_modifier_time } }scope:opposed_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_favorable_report_cons days = short_modifier_time } } The majority report is more convincing. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_hostile_trade_union_report }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_hostile_report_tus days = short_modifier_time } }scope:opposed_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_hostile_report days = short_modifier_time } } |
| A Dying Industry labour_associations_events.4 The government's attacks on the Guild System have spurred riots amongst those who feel their livelihood is under threat. In [ relevant_state state name ], rioting artisans and skilled laborers have smashed machinery and intimidated contractors. A curse on the God to whom our petition We vainly address'd when in starving condition; In vain did we hope, and in vain did we wait, He only derided and mock'd our sad fate,— We're weaving, we're weaving! | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions Right to Associate | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_guild_system OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes turmoil >= 0.1 any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = shopkeepers } any_scope_building = { is_building_group = bg_manufacturing } } | ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = pb_ig } random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes turmoil >= 0.1 any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = shopkeepers } any_scope_building = { is_building_group = bg_manufacturing } } save_scope_as = relevant_state } | If they cannot compete freely, they may find a job elsewhere. scope:relevant_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = large_radicals } state_region = { add_devastation = 10 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_artisans_uprising } Send in the Army to protect property. scope:relevant_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = large_radicals } state_region = { add_devastation = 5 } }scope:pb_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_repressed_artisans_riots days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_artisans_uprising multiplier = 0.35 } |
| The Last Ditch labour_associations_events.5 The efforts of the government to enact [current law] has been met with near-universal opposition from the Trade Unions. Union leadership has called a general strike in a bid to make the government concede. The unions were out in force. Every light pole within a mile of [legislature] was draped in red streamers, and foot traffic was a hopeless endeavor. Several government legislators had elected to sleep in their offices rather than brave the human tides. Others, the radicals in the opposition, had elected to join it. They had found their way back cut off by a line of policemen which snaked all the way around the building. Both sides now saw it as a state of siege. The protestors had worked out a truly impressive logistics system to distribute bread and water amongst themselves, whilst the legislators tapped [legislature]'s cafeteria and wine cellar. "How long?" asked a junior member. His colleague could no longer muster the energy to answer. | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } | Their reaction justifies the bill. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_strikes_stall multiplier = 0.5 }add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time }add_radicals = { pop_type = machinists value = medium_radicals }add_radicals = { pop_type = laborers value = medium_radicals } Offer to negotiate. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_strikes_stall }add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 }add_radicals = { pop_type = machinists value = small_radicals }add_radicals = { pop_type = laborers value = small_radicals } Intercept their means of communication. trigger = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_censorship has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent } }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 } |
| Intolerable Strife labour_associations_events.6 A book detailing the horrific conditions that industrial laborers are routinely subjected to has become an overnight success. The success of the book has raised public interest in [current law], whilst embarrassing the Industrialists. "Thus the social order makes family life almost impossible for the worker. In a comfortless, filthy house, hardly good enough for mere nightly shelter, ill-furnished, often neither rain-tight nor warm, a foul atmosphere filling rooms overcrowded with human beings, no domestic comfort is possible. The husband works the whole day through, perhaps the wife also and the elder children, all in different places; they meet night and morning only, all under perpetual temptation to drink; what family life is possible under such conditions?" |  | Debate | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes OR = { AND = { c:BIC ?= this ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } AND = { NOT = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } } | if = { limit = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } | The workers should be able to advocate for themselves. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_conditions_of_the_working_class }scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_conditions_of_the_working_class_ig days = short_modifier_time } } We must not further upset the owners. add_loyalists = { pop_type = capitalists value = medium_radicals }add_loyalists = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = small_radicals } |
| The Letter of the Law labour_associations_events.7 Emboldened by the government's turn against organized labor, the [ relevant_bourgeois_ig interestgroup name ] have begun to preemptively crack down on labor unions within their shops. The [ unions_ig interestgroup name ] demand a response whilst the old laws still stand. It was an absurdity. The clerk knew it, his superiors knew it, and the union representative standing before him probably knew it as well. A last-minute petition relying upon a law which would vanish from the books before it could ever reach a judge. And yet, it was perfect. It lacked any obvious error that would permit him to reject it out-of-hand. His only option was to let it clog someone else's inbox. The corners of his mouth tightened into a joyless smile. "Thank you, sir. The Ministry will contact you by mail." |  | Debate | enacting_less_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes OR = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_ig } if = { limit = { OR = { ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie.ig_clout > ig:ig_industrialists.ig_clout c:BIC ?= this } } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = relevant_bourgeois_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = relevant_bourgeois_ig } } | They no longer possess any standing. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_tolerated_union_crackdown }scope:relevant_bourgeois_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_tolerated_union_crackdown_ig days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = laborers }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = machinists } We must uphold the existing legislation. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_punished_union_crackdown }scope:relevant_bourgeois_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_punished_union_crackdown_ig days = short_modifier_time } } |
| The Free Market of Labor labour_associations_events.8 As the government takes steps to dismantle the Guild System, emboldened Laborers and Machinists have begun to break into fields they were formerly excluded from. "I'm off. I meant to say earlier. I've found a job in [ relevant_state state cityhubname ], at the factory. They've offered me a loan for transport, a bunk..." He trailed off, looking at the face of his friends in turn. "Thank you. For everything. I'll write, or I'll ask someone to. It's been... it's been wonderful. I'll see you again, someday. I promise." | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions Right to Associate | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_guild_system OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } save_scope_as = relevant_state } | Our efforts are well-rewarded. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_guilds_abolition_benefit } A great victory for liberty. add_modifier = { name = modifier_guilds_abolished_mobility days = normal_modifier_time } |
| Ulterior Motives labour_associations_events.9 Sensing a chance to disadvantage their economic rivals in the cities, prominent members of the Landowners have come out in favor of enacting [current law]. "The unions are agents of disorder - but, crucially, agents which reserve their greatest hostility for our immediate competitors. They will demand concessions from their superiors, concessions which carve deeply into the pockets of [ relevant_state state cityhubname ]'s magnates. To compensate for these losses, they shall be forced to raise their prices, placing our own produce in a deeply favorable position." "And what of our own populace? Need we fear a Union of Peasants?" "They may breathe clean air and feel the sun on their necks. Many hold their own plots. What need have they for a union?" |  | Advance | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes ig:ig_landowners ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_urban_center } } | ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = landowners_ig } ordered_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_urban_center } } order_by = b:building_urban_center.level position = 0 check_range_bounds = no save_scope_as = relevant_state } | A perfectly workable arrangement. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_landowners_assisting_labour_enactment }scope:landowners_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_landowners_assisting_labour days = normal_modifier_time } } We must be wary of offers like these. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_landowners_assisting_labour_enactment multiplier = 0.5 } |
| [Title] Evades Assassination laws.1 Our beloved [title] [ruler] has narrowly survived an assassination attempt. The would-be assassin has ties to the [ jedi_scope interestgroup name ], who oppose the new powers that the [current law] law would bestow upon the [title]. Troubling news of a failed attempt on the life of the [title] has emerged from the capital. Carried out in the dead of night, the [title] was able to defeat the assailant with the aid of [his/her] young protégé. Speaking from a hospital bed, the [title] told the [newspaper] that despite wounds that will surely leave lifelong scars, [his/her] resolve and dedication are stronger than ever. | Autocracy | Advance | any_interest_group = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = jedi_scope } ruler = { save_scope_as = ruler_scope } | For a safe and secure society. add_enactment_phase = 1 scope:ruler_scope = { add_modifier = { name = scarred_and_deformed days = normal_modifier_time } add_trait = scarred } This is how liberty dies. add_enactment_modifier = { name = thunderous_applause }scope:jedi_scope = { add_modifier = { name = hunted_down_and_defeated days = normal_modifier_time is_decaying = yes } } |
| Too Close a Call laws_police.1 An assassin's bullet narrowly missed a minister from the [ assassination_target_ig interestgroup name ] this morning, prompting calls for the rapid passage of [current law] laws. The [ they_dont_like_laws_ig interestgroup name ], opponents of [current law], are also being accused of implicitly supporting the assassin. "This near-miss is just the latest in a long line of incidents. If we don't do something today, then when? Everything within the bounds of the law, of course, although I cannot guarantee our opponents in this matter will view such actions with much glee." |  | Debate | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | every_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = underhanded_ig_scope } random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = underhanded_instigator_ig_scope leader = { save_scope_as = underhanded_instigator_leader_scope } } | Grant [him/her] permission, time is of the essence. add_enactment_modifier = { name = underhanded_tactics }scope:underhanded_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = dishonest_methods days = normal_modifier_time } } No, we do this by the book, or not at all. add_enactment_modifier = { name = by_the_book } |
| Citizens of the World migration_laws.1 Inspired by the ongoing reform to [current law], [ no_borders_visionary character name ] of the [ no_borders_visionary character interestgroup name ] has penned a vision for a future where borders are abolished altogether. "Borders are the scars of a map. Like scars, there are some who find them beautiful, in a way. Hard-fought, hard-earned. They find them so beautiful they forget that maps don't need scars, and so they start inflicting them. On maps, but more horrifyingly, on people. The day we can let go of the past and let the scars heal is the day when we can finally be free." | Migration Controls No Migration Controls | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { NOT = { law_stance = { law = root.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } }NOT = { has_modifier = future_without_borders }NOT = { has_modifier = future_freer_movement } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { leader = { save_scope_as = no_borders_visionary } } | This is the future we want! add_enactment_modifier = { name = future_without_borders }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { leader = { add_modifier = { name = utopian_visionary days = normal_modifier_time } } }ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = anti_border_rhetoric days = normal_modifier_time } } It has the right spirit, but seems naïvely utopian. add_enactment_modifier = { name = future_freer_movement } |
| Foreign Faiths migration_laws.2 The suggestion of [current law] has caused debate among the Devout about whether immigrants will share the uniquely [country-adj] religious sensibilities. "Even if they are [religion], who is to say what strange foreign rites they might bring with them? A bastardized [religion] faith, stained by folk practices from their homeland. What will happen to our own traditions if such things are to be accommodated?" | Migration Controls No Migration Controls | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }ig:ig_devout ?= { NOT = { law_stance = { law = root.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } }NOT = { has_modifier = secular_immigration_policy }NOT = { has_modifier = considering_religious_immigration_restrictions }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_migration_religious_restrictions } } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } | We should accept immigrants who do not practice their faith as we do. add_enactment_modifier = { name = secular_immigration_policy }ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = faithless_hordes days = normal_modifier_time } } Perhaps we can place some restrictions to prevent this. currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_migration_religious_restrictions sponsor = scope:devout_ig cooldown = 120 } }scope:devout_ig = { improve_law_stance = yes } |
| Foreign Competition migration_laws.3 People living in [ improverished_state state name ] under dire conditions are afraid that the [current law] reform will increase competition in the job market. "Whose idea was this? Probably someone who didn't have to worry every day if they would have work, so they would have pay, so they would live. A rich immigrant never seems to be a threat to those who are already well-off. But let me tell you, here down at the bottom, it's always crowded." | Migration Controls No Migration Controls | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_scope_state = { average_sol < 15 }NOT = { has_modifier = free_movement_of_labor }NOT = { has_modifier = welfare_for_all }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_closed_borders value > neutral } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_nativist_labour_market } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { average_sol < 15 } save_scope_as = improverished_state } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_closed_borders value > neutral } } save_scope_as = closed_borders_ig } | If they can't compete, they deserve to lose their jobs. add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_movement_of_labor }scope:improverished_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = medium_radicals strata = lower } } We will ensure that all are cared for, if it comes to that. trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }add_enactment_modifier = { name = welfare_for_all }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_old_age_pension value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = welfare_for_the_world days = normal_modifier_time } } [Country-adj] job-seekers should be prioritized! currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_nativist_labour_market sponsor = scope:closed_borders_ig cooldown = 60 } } |
| Keeping the [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c adjective ] Out migration_laws.4 The effort to institute [current law] has been bolstered by a wave of antipathy towards our neighbors in [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c name ], arguing that the reform is necessary to stop migration from there. "When you say border control, we all know which border you mean. You don't have to say it. You should say it, though. No shame in admitting who we're trying to keep out. We have nothing to be ashamed of. #italic They#! should be ashamed!" | Closed Borders Migration Controls | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_neighbouring_state = { NOT = { owner = root } }NOT = { has_modifier = fear_neighbor_immigration }NOT = { has_modifier = fear_general_immigration } | random_neighbouring_state = { owner = { save_scope_as = immigrant_fear_neighbor } } | Those [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c adjective ] swine are the exact reason for this law! add_enactment_modifier = { name = fear_neighbor_immigration }change_relations = { country = scope:immigrant_fear_neighbor value = -30 } This is a general reform, which has nothing to do with [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c name ]. add_enactment_modifier = { name = fear_general_immigration } |
| People and Goods migration_laws.5 Some free-trade proponents are concerned that the immigration reform towards [current law] is the first steps to closing off [country] from the outside world, not just to migrants but to markets as well. "These may seem like separate issues and principles, but that is a superficial analysis. We are sending a message to the world: You are not welcome here. How can we reject the people of foreign countries and expect them to willingly share with us everything they have to give?" | Closed Borders Migration Controls | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }country_has_isolationism_or_sakoku = no NOT = { has_modifier = trade_restrictions_considered }NOT = { has_modifier = trade_restrictions_rejected }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_migration_tariff_exemptions } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_isolationism_ig } | Perhaps it is! every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = anti_free_trade_rhetoric days = normal_modifier_time } } We will draft the law to make it clear it is not. add_enactment_modifier = { name = trade_restrictions_rejected }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_migration_tariff_exemptions sponsor = scope:anti_isolationism_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| Plea of the [ migrant_pop culture name ] migration_laws.6 In response to the proposal of [current law], [ migrant_pop culture name ] people living in [ migrant_pop_state state name ] have pointed out that they once immigrated there as well and that future restrictions may prevent relatives from joining them. "We have all arrived where we are at some point. Some yesterday, some in long-forgotten times. Perhaps forgotten for a reason. How much blood was spilled when your people claimed the land you now call yours? Where do you get the moral authority to tell us who has the right to join us here?" | Closed Borders Migration Controls | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_scope_pop = { state = { save_temporary_scope_as = m_state } culture = { NOT = { has_homeland = scope:m_state } NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = root } } }NOT = { has_modifier = no_family_immigration }NOT = { has_modifier = family_reunification_discussion } | random_scope_pop = { limit = { state = { save_temporary_scope_as = m_state } culture = { NOT = { has_homeland = scope:m_state } NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = root } } } culture = { save_scope_as = migrant_pop } state = { save_scope_as = migrant_pop_state } } | Part of the point is to stop everyone from inviting their family here! add_radicals = { value = large_radicals culture = scope:migrant_pop }add_enactment_modifier = { name = no_family_immigration } This is not about the [ migrant_pop culture name ] at all. add_enactment_modifier = { name = family_reunification_discussion } |
| Popular Acclamation monarchy_law_events.1 A procession of activists in the capital have been joined by soldiers and civilians, all calling for monarchy. [ monarchist_ig_leader_scope character name ] has taken the opportunity to speak directly to the assembled crowd. "Friends! The nation cries out for leadership, for justice, and for an end to chaos! | Imported Bluebloods? monarchy_law_events.3 The question of who should actually sit on the throne has prompted some monarchists to suggest inviting a member of the [ neighboring_monarchy country adjective ] royal family over to serve as our monarch. This idea has been received, to put it lightly, contentiously. "It's simple, really. | The Gold-Foil Crown monarchy_law_events.4 Some monarchists have been discussing what the royal regalia ought to be, and it has led to a realization that the country has nothing that really seems appropriate for a monarch. One attempt at a homemade crown, made from tin and gold foil, has been seized as a symbol of the proposed monarchy's 'innately tawdy' nature. "Our beautiful monarchy—for a monarchy we must have—calls for the certain dignity of modest and charming regalia. To this end, I have already contacted several goldsmiths, gemcutters, jewelers, and a shop that can make a proper throne seat with what they tell me is fine Corinthian leather. | The Streets Alive single_party_state.3 Condemning the efforts of the [ruling party] to establish a [current law], protesters largely affiliated with the [ protester_ig interestgroup name ] have begun a multi-day occupation of the streets in cities across [capital state]. The streets of [capital city] were a sea of voices, slogans waxing and waning as the organizers conducted the chorus. Their voices would echo through the streets, reverberating through windows, reflected in the badges of the policemen which formed their lines in front of them. Tonight, the streets of [country] were alive - but something else could be seen in the eyes of every protester. | Halting the Revolution single_party_state.4 The efforts of the [ruling party] to liquidate the remaining bourgeois parties in [country] have been frustrated by fierce resistance from far-right elements sympathetic to the [ fascist_ig interestgroup name ]. The soot had covered the front of the [ruling party]'s headquarters, deposited from the short-lived inferno released by the flaming oil that had been thrown against the facade. Broken glass crunched underfoot, as militiamen moved to secure the building once more. It was clear that this was not a simple act of vandalism.
