| Society of the Godless state_atheism.2 [ godless_pop pop name ] at the [ godless_uni building name ] of [ godless_state state name ] are strong supporters of the [current law] Law. They have founded the Society of the Godless as a means of promoting anti-religious thought. "I have heard us denigrated as thugs 'encroaching on the highest achievement of human culture'. If that is the highest that humankind has achieved, then I must laugh—to reach a greater height, all I would have to do is hop over a puddle." | State Atheism | Advance | any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes has_building = building_university any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = academics } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes has_building = building_university any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = academics } } save_scope_as = godless_state random_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_university } save_scope_as = godless_uni } random_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = academics } save_scope_as = godless_pop } } ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = godless_ig leader = { save_scope_as = godless_leader } } | An enlightened initiative. add_enactment_modifier = { name = society_of_the_godless_mod multiplier = 1 } Accost the theists on the streets. add_enactment_modifier = { name = society_of_the_godless_mod multiplier = 1.5 }scope:godless_state = { add_loyalists_in_state = { pop_type = academics value = very_large_radicals } add_radicals_in_state = { religion = root.religion value = medium_radicals } } Elevate the Society in the universities. trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no leader = { NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist } } } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = society_of_the_godless_mod }custom_tooltip = { text = godless_ig_leader create_character = { ig_leader = yes interest_group = ig_intelligentsia religion = rel:atheist ideology = ideology_nihilist } } |
| Works of Charity state_atheism.3 Charities associated with the [ works_of_charity_ig interestgroup name ] have come under fire by supporters of the [current law] Law for being staffed by clergy and pious laity. Many people have rallied to the defense of these popular charities, threatening the success of [current law]. "I don't care how many people call it a 'charity'. It's a religious sect, and it has as much an agenda as any other sect out there. It doesn't matter if they say they've changed—you and I both know they haven't! The public be damned, we have to go after these people." | State Atheism | Stall | any_interest_group = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = works_of_charity_ig } | Maintain the attacks on the charities. add_enactment_modifier = { name = unpopular_attacks_on_charities } Promote state welfare to undercut them. trigger = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_no_social_security } }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = state_welfare_disparaged } } 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = state_welfare_promoted } } } Set up competing relief charities to undercut them. trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_no_social_security }add_modifier = { name = ad_hoc_government_charities days = short_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = imitating_charities } Just let the charities be. scope:works_of_charity_ig = { add_modifier = { name = popular_works_of_charity days = short_modifier_time } } |
| Extraterritoriality in God's Nation state_atheism.4 Attempting to expel missionaries, clerics, faith healers and the like has caused an uproar abroad. [ missionary_nation country name ] has resisted the forced extradition of their citizens in [country]. "No, I will not abide these laws! I am a man of [divinity], and I will not be bullied out of my noble purpose. You could drag me out in manacles and I would still be legally entitled to enter this place. If I am forced to, I shall invoke extraterritoriality upon this farce!" | State Atheism | Stall | any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } }any_country = { any_neighbouring_state = { owner = root } NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism } is_country_type = recognized } | random_country = { limit = { any_neighbouring_state = { owner = root } NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism } is_country_type = recognized } save_scope_as = missionary_nation } ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = atheist_ig } | We should rethink this, before we have a war. scope:atheist_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_caved_to_theists days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_tolerance } Force them out if you have to. create_incident = { country = scope:missionary_nation value = 10 }scope:missionary_nation = { change_relations = { country = root value = -15 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_infamy } They can stay if they swear off their faith. ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_expelled_devouts_friends days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = clergymen }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_devout } |
| A River's New Bearing state_atheism.5 As [country] embarks on a quest of questioning traditional religious morality, members of the Intelligentsia present their theses. "Furthermore, I should note that if God is to be truly dead, God must be absent from worship. Let us be doomed to freedom, that we may write our history. Not in stone or in blood, as our history under God has been, but in ink and in deeds. God cannot be—and should not be accepted any longer as—a convenient excuse, a lie to embolden the whims of the powerful!" | State Atheism | Debate | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no leader = { OR = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist } } } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig } ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } | Tradition still yet has a place in society. scope:devout_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_retreat_on_faith days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_great_retreat_on_faith } Away with faith. Away with all of its trappings. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_atheist_progressivism }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals interest_group = scope:devout_ig } There is nothing at all that should not be questioned. trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = scope:devout_ig } |
| Golden Indulgences state_atheism.6 The Armed Forces have begun carrying out seizures from every [ majority_religion pop religion name ] place of worship in [ religion_seizures_state state name ]. Every structure, artwork, and piece of gold kitsch are being tallied for liquidation. "Right, that's just what's in the presbytery. What's he got in his quarters?" "Sir, we pulled all this from one of the ambulatory chapels." "Bugger me, there's enough gold in there to start a new mine. Call up four more men and search the remaining chapels, on the double!" | State Atheism | Advance | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { ig_approval >= 0 ig_counts_as_marginal = no } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_ig } random_scope_pop = { limit = { religion = root.religion } save_scope_as = majority_religion } ordered_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } order_by = state_population position = 0 check_range_bounds = no save_scope_as = religion_seizures_state } | Melt the gold and re-mint it. scope:religion_seizures_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = medium_radicals religion = root.religion } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals religion = root.religion }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_seizure_of_church_gold_minting multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium } Allow our brave forces some 'danger pay.' scope:religion_seizures_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = medium_radicals religion = root.religion } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_seizure_of_church_gold_army } Give the clerics a stipend to start new lives. scope:religion_seizures_state = { add_loyalists_in_state = { value = small_radicals strata = lower } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_seizure_of_church_gold_charity } |
| Taxation Is Theft tax.1 A fringe group of the [ antitax_rebels politicalmovement name ] has gained remarkable traction. They advocate for the abolition of all taxation, equating it to an act of theft by the state. Their intent seems to be to block any tax reform other than total abolition. "As a private and sovereign [country-adj] citizen, I do not consent to the pillaging of my hard-earned coffers by the so-called 'government'. It is a violation not only of my rights, but of the free market, the very foundation of basic economics." | Graduated Taxation Land-Based Taxation Per-Capita Taxation Proportional Taxation | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation is_enacting_law = law_type:law_land_based_taxation is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation }any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = taxation_is_theft }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts } } | random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = antitax_rebels } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = antitax_ig } | But who would build the roads? add_enactment_modifier = { name = taxation_is_theft multiplier = 3 }scope:antitax_rebels = { add_modifier = { name = antitax_anger days = long_modifier_time } } Promise short-term tax cuts. add_enactment_modifier = { name = taxation_is_theft }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_short_term_tax_cuts sponsor = scope:antitax_ig cooldown = 30 } } |

| Philanthropy tax.5 The Upper strata have decided to donate generously to the poor. Many among them even advocate for the [current law] law, agreeing that it would be a fair means of redistributing wealth. "We have so much, and they have so little. We are still wealthy even when we give generously, are you really going to miss a few thousand Pounds? | Temporary Embarrassment tax.6 Ambitious Middle strata citizens are opposing [current law] on the grounds that when they inevitably become wealthy capitalists, they will be excessively taxed. | Public Meltdown technocracy.1 Following a series of poor business decisions, [ technocrat_leader character name ] has taken to lashing out against [his/her] detractors in the tabloids. These screeds have served to destroy [his/her] public reputation and damaged support for Technocracy. "Low passions be praised in this theater of the damned! An era of unenlightened barbarism and puritanical populism. Only the truly mad could ever reject the benevolent rule of the enlightened few. Damned, lost sheep I name you all! Perhaps you should remain there, while I and my fellows would reap the benefits of a matrix so utopian that all would quake beneath its magnificence. And you will #bold beg #!to be part of it then! Mark my words, gentlemen!" | Technocracy | Stall | any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no OR = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia } any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = capitalists } law_stance = { law = law_type:law_technocracy value >= approve } leader = { is_immortal = no } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no OR = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia } any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = capitalists } law_stance = { law = law_type:law_technocracy value >= approve } leader = { is_immortal = no } } save_scope_as = technocrat_ig leader = { save_scope_as = technocrat_leader } } | A public bailout will resolve the matter. add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 10% name = foolish_technocrat multiplier = 2 }add_modifier = { name = bailed_out_technocrat multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small days = short_modifier_time }scope:technocrat_leader = { add_modifier = { # lose popularity name = disgraced_technocrat } } Disgraceful behavior. add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 20% name = foolish_technocrat multiplier = 4 }scope:technocrat_leader = { add_modifier = { # lose popularity name = disgraced_technocrat } } Have [him/her] committed to the asylum. trigger = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent has_law = law_type:law_secret_police scope:technocrat_leader = { OR = { has_trait = senile has_trait = syphilis has_trait = psychological_affliction } } } }add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 5% name = foolish_technocrat }scope:technocrat_leader = { retire_character = yes } |
| The New Machine technocracy.2 A group of influential $engineers$ have greatly improved productivity in the [ new_machine_building building name ] of [ new_machine_state state name ] through the application of scientific principles. They suggest that under Technocracy they would be able to apply these principles to the mechanicisms of government. A salient point of intelligence is understanding one's limitations. Through this comes the recognition that one's experience is finite and subject to perspective. Without this pillar, human intelligence is prone to hubris. For example, someone may conceive the idea that it is possible to treat bureaucracy as a machine, rather than the cluster of skills that it is. Thusly, treat humans as problems to solve, rather than independent variables with experience and intelligence of their own. | Technocracy | Advance | any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = engineers OR = { pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry } } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = engineers OR = { pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry } } } save_scope_as = new_machine_state random_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = engineers OR = { pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry } } save_scope_as = new_machine_pop workplace = { save_scope_as = new_machine_building } } } | The industrial sector needs them most. add_enactment_modifier = { name = new_machine_enactment }scope:new_machine_state = { add_modifier = { name = new_machine_state_mod days = normal_modifier_time } } A fascinating proposal. trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 scope:new_machine_building = { add_modifier = { name = new_machine_building_mod days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Elitists! technocracy.3 Debate rages between $academics$ at the University of [ university_state state name ] over the issue of popular governance versus rule by an enlightened elite. The argument has captured public attention due to the ongoing effort to establish a Technocracy. "Unless, said I, either philosophers become kings in our states or those whom we now call our kings and rulers take to the pursuit of philosophy seriously and adequately, and there is a conjunction of these two things, political power and philosophic intellgence, while the motley horde of the natures who at present pursue either apart from the other are compulsory excluded, there can be no cessation of troubles, dear Glaucon, for our states, nor, I fancy, for the human race either." | Technocracy | Debate | any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes has_building = building_university } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes has_building = building_university } save_scope_as = university_state random_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_university } save_scope_as = university_building } } | The people need great minds to see what is best for them. add_enactment_modifier = { name = technocracy_debate_enactment multiplier = 2 }scope:university_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { pop_type = academics value = very_large_radicals } } We can at least agree on our distaste for $aristocrats$. add_enactment_modifier = { # 5% name = technocracy_debate_enactment }add_modifier = { name = anti_aristocratic_sentiment days = normal_modifier_time } |
| Miracle Workers theocracy.1 The below description is one of several available for this event. Patients at the [capital city] Charity Hospital are recovering rapidly. They attribute their recovery to miracles performed by visiting clergy who are patrons of the hospital. Such displays are encouraging support for [current law] throughout [country] "Think first of the good of others before yourselves. To give is divine, to cultivate a generous spirit is true virtue. Follow the path of righteousness not out of fear, but out of love." | State Religion Theocracy | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion }NOT = { has_modifier = miracle_workers_mod } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = priests_scope } capital = { save_scope_as = capital_scope } | Religious institutions are the backbone of the state. trigger = { OR = { AND = { has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system institution_investment_level = { institution = institution_health_system value >= 2 } } AND = { has_law = law_type:law_religious_schools institution_investment_level = { institution = institution_schools value >= 2 } } } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system institution_investment_level = { institution = institution_health_system value >= 2 } } capital = { add_modifier = { name = miraculous_healing days = normal_modifier_time } } # success chance add_enactment_modifier = { name = miracle_workers_mod } }else = { capital = { add_modifier = { name = religious_education_modifier days = normal_modifier_time } } # success chance add_enactment_modifier = { name = miracle_workers_mod multiplier = 3 } } The [ priests_scope interestgroup name ] are true followers of the divine. add_enactment_modifier = { name = miracle_workers_mod multiplier = 2 }ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = miracle_workers_ig_mod days = normal_modifier_time } } [Current law] will fill our hearts with faith. add_enactment_modifier = { name = miracle_workers_mod multiplier = 4 } |
| The Blasphemy Laws theocracy.2 As part of the transition to [current law], the [ priests_scope interestgroup name ] have introduced and enforced a series of harsh anti-blasphemy laws in [ blasphemy_state state name ]. These measures are polarizing even among the faithful. "It is as the scriptures say: ours is the only path of the divine. We can suffer no deviation from this path, no defamation of our edicts or of our church. For the faithful the reward is eternal; so too is the punishment for the blasphemer." | State Religion Theocracy | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { religion = { is_state_religion = ROOT } } }NOR = { has_modifier = blasphemy_laws has_modifier = relaxed_blasphemy_laws }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_draconian_blasphemy_laws } } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = priests_scope } random_scope_state = { limit = { any_scope_pop = { religion = { is_state_religion = ROOT } } } save_scope_as = blasphemy_state } | We must uphold the law. add_enactment_modifier = { name = blasphemy_laws }random_list = { 50 = { scope:blasphemy_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { religion = ROOT.religion value = medium_radicals } } } 50 = { scope:blasphemy_state = { add_loyalists_in_state = { religion = ROOT.religion value = medium_radicals } } } } The laws should be relaxed. add_enactment_modifier = { name = relaxed_blasphemy_laws } Blasphemers shall be put to death. trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_draconian_blasphemy_laws sponsor = scope:priests_scope cooldown = 60 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = blasphemy_laws }scope:blasphemy_state = { add_loyalists_in_state = { religion = ROOT.religion value = 0.1 } } |
| Religious Pogroms theocracy.3 Pogroms targeting [religion] people living in [ pogrom_state state name ] are being perpetrated by fanatical [ perpetrator_pop pop religion name ] supporters of the [current law] law. "They came at night. We were lucky; they came for our neighbors first and we heard them smash the glass and ransack the shop, so we hid under the floorboards. Huddled in the dark, I pressed my hand to my children's mouths so they would not cry out. We were silent and still. We didn't dare emerge until dawn." | State Religion Theocracy | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { PREV = { state_religious_acceptance = { target = PREV.religion value < religious_acceptance_medium } } } any_scope_pop = { religion = ROOT.religion pop_has_primary_culture = yes } }NOR = { has_modifier = pogroms_law_success has_modifier = pogroms_law_time } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { PREV = { state_religious_acceptance = { target = PREV.religion value < religious_acceptance_medium } } } any_scope_pop = { religion = ROOT.religion pop_has_primary_culture = yes } } save_scope_as = pogrom_state random_scope_pop = { limit = { religion = ROOT.religion pop_has_primary_culture = yes } save_scope_as = perpetrator_pop } random_scope_pop = { limit = { PREV = { state_religious_acceptance = { target = PREV.religion value < religious_acceptance_medium } } } save_scope_as = target_pop religion = { save_scope_as = target_religion } } } | We cannot interfere. add_enactment_modifier = { name = pogroms_law_success }scope:pogrom_state = { add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = { religion = scope:target_religion multiplier = -4 days = short_modifier_time } } This is not the [ perpetrator_pop pop religion name ] way. add_enactment_modifier = { name = pogroms_law_time multiplier = 2 }scope:pogrom_state = { add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = { religion = scope:target_religion multiplier = -2 days = short_modifier_time } } This will not stand. Arrest the perpetrators. trigger = { institution_investment_level = { institution = institution_police value >= 3 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = pogroms_law_time }scope:pogrom_state = { add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = { religion = scope:target_religion multiplier = -1 days = short_modifier_time } } |
| Legislative Failures unable_to_enact.1 Despite many efforts on the part of the government to successfully revive [current law] from its present imbroglio, the law has come to be regarded as nothing but an inevitable failure. "#italic That#! bill? Please, don't talk to me about it." "I would say it's better to just pretend it never happened, don't you agree?" "Certainly, but that won't stop that fool from asking about it every day..." |  | Country_event | NOT = { has_variable = legislative_failures_event }enacting_any_law = yes enactment_chance <= 0 num_pending_events:enactment = 0 | set_variable = { name = legislative_failures_event days = 100 } | We must sponsor a complete rewrite of the bill. trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one enactment_chance_without_enactment_modifier > 0 }add_enactment_setback = 1 clear_enactment_modifier = yes remove_variable = legislative_failures_event Off to the fire with the entire notion, then. trigger = { OR = { enactment_setback_count > 1 enactment_chance_without_enactment_modifier <= 0 } }fallback = yes cancel_enactment = yes remove_variable = legislative_failures_event |
| The Legislative Graveyard unable_to_enact.2 After a series of costly and embarassing setbacks, the sitting [country-adj] government has decided to halt its pursuit of [current law]. It will be some time before political will exists to try again. Defeat lingered in the air, along with the stench burnt tobacco and spoiling liquor. Hopes crushed, ideas splintered, and desire spoiled. For better or worse, the message was clear: nobody wanted [current law] but the men in the room. A long, treacherous path lay ahead to reingratiate the idea. |  | Country_event |  |  | Unfortunate, and embarassing. cancel_enactment = yes |
| Houses of Faith welfare_laws.1 The prospect of workhouses including religious instruction in their work has allowed the proposal of [current law] to gain favor with the Devout. "What is the difference between the man who is pursued by poverty to his own destruction and the ascetic who pursues poverty for the betterment of his soul? The answer surely lies not in the nature of material poverty, but in poverty of the spirit. This is the form of poverty that the workhouses most desperately need to alleviate." | Poor Laws | Advance | NOT = { has_modifier = promise_religious_workhouses }NOT = { has_modifier = promise_secular_workhouses }any_scope_pop = { state = { state_religious_acceptance = { target = PREV.religion value < religious_acceptance_low } } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_religious_workhouses } } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } random_scope_pop = { limit = { state = { state_religious_acceptance = { target = PREV.religion value < religious_acceptance_low } } } religion = { save_scope_as = discriminated_religion_scope } } | Religious practices are key to the moral discipline the poor need! add_enactment_modifier = { name = promise_religious_workhouses }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_religious_workhouses sponsor = scope:devout_ig cooldown = 120 } }add_radicals = { value = large_radicals religion = scope:discriminated_religion_scope } Moral principles can be instilled without the help of the Devout. add_enactment_modifier = { name = promise_secular_workhouses } |
| The Principle of Population welfare_laws.2 Some intellectuals are warning that if the [current law] are not passed, an increasing population of unproductive poor will become an unmaintainable burden on society. "A growing poor population is an inevitability in a society which does not attempt to control it. Some measures may seem callous to the short-sighted, but if the poor are not discouraged from multiplying, we shall soon have more starving mouths." | Poor Laws | Debate | NOT = { has_modifier = malthusian_arguments }NOT = { has_modifier = anti_malthusian_arguments }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_malthusian_foundations } } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = malthusian_ig } | A firm hand is required to stave off such disaster. add_enactment_modifier = { name = malthusian_arguments }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_malthusian_foundations sponsor = scope:malthusian_ig cooldown = 120 } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals strata = lower } This fearmongering makes the law seem outright inhumane. add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_malthusian_arguments } We must take the time to make better arguments known. trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_malthusian_arguments multiplier = -1 }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals strata = upper } |
| Prisons of the Poor welfare_laws.3 Some social reformers among the Intelligentsia have spoken out against the proposed [current law], viewing them as an attempt to make poverty itself a crime. "If I may bring your attention to the design of the most ambitious workhouse plans, you may notice a striking similarity to our most modern attempts at constructing ideal prisons. This is because they are prisons. Prisons intended to house people who have committed no crime, where the purpose is not justice, but punishment and exploitation of the weak for its own ends." | Poor Laws | Stall | NOT = { has_modifier = workhouses_prison_comparison }NOT = { has_modifier = workhouse_plan_reevaluation }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_workhouse_regulations } } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig } | The poor are a burden on society, a crime in itself. add_enactment_modifier = { name = workhouses_prison_comparison }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = criminalization_of_poverty days = normal_modifier_time } } There must be ways for this new system to respect personal liberty. add_enactment_modifier = { name = workhouse_plan_reevaluation }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_workhouse_regulations sponsor = scope:intelligentsia_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| Child of the Workhouse welfare_laws.4 A child who grew up in a workhouse institution spoke before [legislature] today, detailing the conditions there and urging the abolition of the Poor Laws. "Most of you listeners would, I am sure, hesitate to bring your child to visit such a place. Yet children are nevertheless there, every day, although they learn quickly to be less like children, to be less a part of a family and more a part in a machine. Family being, like food, merely another luxury that no amount of work seems to make an inmate deserving of in these places." | No Social Security Old Age Pension Wage Subsidies | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_poor_laws OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_social_security is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }NOT = { has_modifier = child_testinomies } NOT = { has_modifier = child_testimony } |  | More of these children must be heard! add_enactment_modifier = { name = child_testinomies }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_child_labor_allowed value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = workhouse_children_outrage days = short_modifier_time } } This makes the point well enough. add_enactment_modifier = { name = child_testimony } |
| A Guiding Hand welfare_laws.5 The Landowners are concerned that, without the workhouses watching over the poor, they will be lacking the guidance they see as necessary for the lower classes. "In any society, the aristocracy has a certain responsibility towards the lower classes, as they too have a responsibility towards their betters. When we abolish an institution designed to facilitate such a responsibility, we must take care that we do not abolish the responsibility with it." | No Social Security Old Age Pension Wage Subsidies | Debate | has_law = law_type:law_poor_laws OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_social_security is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }NOT = { has_modifier = paternalist_argument }NOT = { has_modifier = anti_paternalist_argument } | ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = landowners_ig } | The poor are no more in need of guidance than the rich. add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_paternalist_argument }ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = anti_paternalist_sentiment days = normal_modifier_time } } The whip cracks out of love! add_enactment_modifier = { name = paternalist_argument } |
| Idle Hands welfare_laws.6 The closing of workhouses with the abolition of the Poor Laws has some citizens concerned about increased crime rates as the poorest members of society will no longer be contained in these institutions. "If someone suggested the abolition of a leper colony, surely the first question they'd be expected to answer would be: Where will these people go? Yet we are expected to assume that the destitute and desperate pouring out from closing workhouses should not be cause for such concerns. These are people who could not make an honest living. What dishonest living will they make once they're out?" | No Social Security Old Age Pension Wage Subsidies | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_poor_laws OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_social_security is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_no_police value < neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = drifter_concerns_dismissed } NOT = { has_modifier = drifter_concerns_accommodated } |  | This fear of the poor is exactly what caused this inhumane law! add_enactment_modifier = { name = drifter_concerns_dismissed }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_no_police value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = vagrants_and_wastrels days = normal_modifier_time } } These people have a right to feel safe from indigent drifters. add_enactment_modifier = { name = drifter_concerns_accommodated } |
| The Price of War welfare_laws.8 Old veterans have spoken out in favor of [current law], rallying many in the Armed Forces to support the reform. "I cannot emphasize enough how much of the brutality of war on the human mind comes down to uncertainty. Uncertainty about when the enemy will attack, uncertainty about where you will wake up days from now, uncertainty about #ITALIC if#! you will wake up days from now and about who will still be with you if you do. Many feel uncertain about what will happen to them if they die. All we ask is the relief of having some certainty about what will happen to us if we live." | Old Age Pension | Advance | NOT = { has_modifier = veteran_support }NOT = { has_modifier = general_support }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_veterans_pensions } } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_ig } | Military veterans are our highest priority in this new pension system. add_enactment_modifier = { name = veteran_support }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_veterans_pensions sponsor = scope:armed_forces_ig cooldown = 120 } }ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = veterans_prioritized days = normal_modifier_time } }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = veterans_prioritized days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = -1 } } All workers serve their country in their own way. add_enactment_modifier = { name = general_support } |
| Unionized Leverage welfare_laws.9 The Industrialists are concerned about the proposed [current law] reform giving the Trade Unions leverage in benefits negotiations. "Charity is all well and good, but the idea that there are certain forms of charity one is entitled to simply by existing can be quite insidious. A fair negotiation allows the parties to come to an agreement based on what they need from each other. If one party is already guaranteed certain provisions, well, you've already given them an inch for free, haven't you? And you know what they say about giving them an inch." | Old Age Pension Wage Subsidies | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { # To account for woke capitalism law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = pro_union_argument }NOT = { has_modifier = anti_union_argument }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_welfare_pro_employer_bias } } | ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } | All the better. These should be rights, not benefits. add_enactment_modifier = { name = pro_union_argument }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { add_modifier = { name = embraced_union_leverage days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = -2 } }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = embraced_union_leverage days = normal_modifier_time } } We will work with business owners to draft acceptable reform. currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_welfare_pro_employer_bias sponsor = scope:industrialists_ig cooldown = 120 } }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { improve_law_stance = yes add_modifier = { name = repudiated_union_leverage days = normal_modifier_time } }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = repudiated_union_leverage days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = -1 } } |
| [Title] Urges Caution welfare_laws.10 [Title] [ruler] has recently held a speech warning against government overspending, making [his/her] supporters in the [ruler IG] more skeptical of the proposed [current law]. "[Country]'s institutions must be built to last, through good and bad times alike. We must take care that we do not, during a mild winter, gain the ambition to expand our house so that we cannot afford to heat it during a harsh one." | Old Age Pension Wage Subsidies | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }NOT = { has_modifier = rejected_austerity_argument }NOT = { has_modifier = accepted_austerity_argument }tax_level > medium ruler = { interest_group ?= { NOT = { law_stance = { law = root.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } } } |  | This proposal is about sharing the wealth, not diminishing it. add_enactment_modifier = { name = rejected_austerity_argument }ruler = { interest_group ?= { add_modifier = { name = overspending_on_welfare days = normal_modifier_time } } } It is important for [country] to live within its means. add_enactment_modifier = { name = accepted_austerity_argument } |
| Too Much Democracy? ig_petitions.1 Men associated with the influential Landowners have approached the government, seeking reforms. They argue that voting rights have expanded too much, too quickly, and ask the government to consider rolling back the passing of the [current law] law. "The masses can support their betters if they wish, but they should not be involved in matters that are none of their business." | Universal Suffrage | Debate | ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = landowners_petition_var }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_voter_suppression_policies } } | ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = landowners_ig } set_variable = { name = landowners_petition_var days = normal_modifier_time } | We shall address their grievances. currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_voter_suppression_policies sponsor = scope:landowners_ig cooldown = 60 } }scope:landowners_ig = { improve_law_stance = yes add_modifier = { name = tarnished_reputation_modifier #pop attraction bad days = normal_modifier_time } } Restricting the franchise? | The Faith of the Land ig_petitions.4 Followers of the Devout have expressed their support to the government efforts to recognize the beliefs of the faithful as the one true church of the state and formally implement a [current law]. "In the darkest part of the night, faith shines, immaculate on its conception, like a mutating, everlasting candle. Now yellow, now orange, reddish. What other beacon will ever lead us? The priest has always been so fervent in his words. His whole body agitates, possessed by the sermon, his face gets brighter, brighter!" | State Religion | Advance | ig:ig_devout ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = devout_petition_var } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } set_variable = { name = devout_petition_var days = normal_modifier_time } | This will greatly support our cause. add_enactment_modifier = { name = devout_state_support_modifier #law enactment chance }ig:ig_devout ?= { #but they get more powerful add_modifier = { name = devout_state_influence_modifier #pol str days = normal_modifier_time } } We cannot let the Devout become too influential. ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = rejected_petition days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Freedom of Expression? ig_petitions.7 [ intelligentsia_leader character name ], leader of the Intelligentsia, has publicly supported the government efforts to pass the [current law] law. However, there are some that think [ intelligentsia_leader character shehe ]'s only doing it for [ intelligentsia_leader character herhis ] own benefit. The scholar has been sitting all day in that velvet lined chair. He mutters about what's going on in the east. Something is always going on in the east. "Stopping the people from talking does not stop the people from talking. It stops the people." | Right of Assembly | Advance | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = intelligentsia_petition_var } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig leader = { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_leader } } set_variable = { name = intelligentsia_petition_var days = normal_modifier_time } | It would be good to ensure [ intelligentsia_leader character shehe ] gets the necessary support. scope:intelligentsia_leader = { add_modifier = { name = intelligentsia_free_speech_leader_modifier #popularity days = normal_modifier_time } } We cannot personalize the law in just one person. add_enactment_modifier = { name = intelligentsia_free_speech_modifier #success chance }scope:intelligentsia_leader = { add_modifier = { name = intelligentsia_free_speech_leader_bad_modifier #popularity days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Bring Our Boys Home! ig_petitions.10 The [ rural_folk_ig interestgroup name ] are already looking ahead to the next harvest. The draft regularly draws away the most able hands away from the farm just when they're needed most. A delegation has arrived, asking for a stop to the [current law] law. Their sturdy hands are sweaty, sometimes they shake. "No, no, it was only your imagination, good Sir, we are quite calm here, you see, but we do worry. Calmly, of course, but we worry." The war is always going badly, they say, doesn't have to be the same with the harvest. | Mass Conscription Peasant Levies | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies }ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = rural_petition_var } | ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { save_scope_as = rural_folk_ig } random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } save_scope_as = conscription_state } set_variable = { name = rural_petition_var days = normal_modifier_time } | We shall address their grievances. scope:conscription_state = { add_modifier = { #less conscription name = less_conscription_rural_folk_modifier days = normal_modifier_time } }add_modifier = { name = accepted_petition days = normal_modifier_time } The military needs men more than the farm needs hands. add_enactment_modifier = { name = rural_folk_conscription_concerns_ignored_modifier }ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { add_modifier = { name = rejected_petition days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Restricted Education ig_petitions.13 Representatives of the Trade Unions have approached the government, expressing their disappointment in the passing of the [current law] law. "They have brought their kids with them. Oh, [divinity], that reminds me of why I hate children. So loud, my head, so loud. No amount of education will ever fix these ones, that's for sure. "Yes, yes, we hear you, we absolutely hear you. Stay behind the desk, please, we're processing your petition." #italic Sigh#!." | No Schools Private Schools Religious Schools | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools }has_technology_researched = labor_movement ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = trade_unions_petititon_var } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } set_variable = { name = trade_unions_petititon_var days = normal_modifier_time } | We should calm these people before we go ahead. add_enactment_modifier = { name = trade_unions_placation_modifier #enactment time malus }capital = { add_modifier = { name = accepted_petition_TU #loyalists from sol days = normal_modifier_time } } We cannot let the public opinion have an impact in our decisions. add_enactment_modifier = { #enactment bonus name = no_public_schools_modifier }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = rejected_petition days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Tax Reforms ig_petitions.16 A group of influential Industrialists have approached the government, unhappy with the proposed tax system, which they say places an undue burden on the most successful members of society, and seek the repeal of the [current law] law. "You are creating a nation of loafers and slackers, a nation where a Lazybones Plaza shall promptly be erected in honor of the proud habitants of our streets!" The man's carotid is about to explode. A healthy blush now shines in his cheeks, like a modest dancer. A doctor watches in attentive silence. Fascinating. | Graduated Taxation | Debate | ig:ig_industrialists ?= { is_powerful = yes is_in_government = no }NOT = { has_variable = industrialists_petition_var } | ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } set_variable = { name = industrialists_petition_var days = normal_modifier_time } | Success should not be punished! add_enactment_modifier = { name = industralists_tax_agreed_modifier }add_modifier = { name = accepted_petition_IN days = normal_modifier_time } They only want the rich to get richer. add_enactment_modifier = { name = industralists_tax_denied_modifier }ig:ig_industrialists ?= { add_modifier = { name = rejected_petition days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| Services to the Nation ig_petitions.19 Officers from the Armed Forces have expressed their support to the enactment of the new [current law] law. However, some have also raised their concerns about this lowering the overall quality of the army. "These officers have a different air to them. Everyone says so. And by everyone they mean the ladies from the countryside. The young ones, especially. Someone's niece ran away with one of them and the scandal reached even the capital. Her uncle was the earl, they say." Military men watch, leaning against the railings that border the fields. | The [ homesteading_act_state_scope state name ] Homestead Act land_ownership_law_events.1 Claiming that [ homesteading_act_state_scope state name ]'s prime lands should be settled by virtous yeomen like themselves, the [current law] law proposal is experiencing a surge of popularity among members of the [ rural_folks_scope interestgroup name ] eying an opportunity in the frontiers—to the great chagrin of the [ landowners_scope interestgroup name ] currently dominating the agricultural sector. Debates raged for days in the courthouse. Accusations flew as fast as declarations of this ancestral heritage or that natural right. | The Shackled Soul of the Nation land_ownership_law_events.2 In a somewhat surprising move, claiming that [country-adj] [ law_serfdom getlawtype name ] has driven a wedge between the spiritual backbone of the country, the [ rural_folks_scope interestgroup name ], and an increasingly foreign-influenced upper class, a number of conservative thinkers have come out in support of [current law]—breaking ranks with their more traditionally inclined fellows in the [ landowners_scope interestgroup name ] and the Devout. "Our nation, in its current state, is made up of two social groups that have very little in common. My fellow members of the [ landowners_scope interestgroup name ], who wear foreign clothes, follow foreign trends, some even speaking in foreign tongues, have become too detatched from their own [country-adj] heritage—their [country-adj]-ness little more than a family name and a sworn loyalty to the [title]. | The Plight of the Second Sons land_ownership_law_events.4 With virgin land to award second sons becoming an increasingly rare commodity in [ commercialized_agriculture_state_scope state name ], many farmers engaged in the local [ commercialized_agriculture_building_scope building name ] hope that a transition to [current law] will yield the revenues necessary to provide for all their offspring upon death—or at the very least leave a single profitable farm that can be sold off for profit. The fields are not wholly sustainable within new models. Each farmer would be left to his or her own devices regarding each field. It is assumed that each subsequent harvest would overwork the soil. Middle-management to ensure a smooth transition process is required. Appropriate training for each farmer looking to exploit the new [current law] laws is also required. | Farmer's Co-op Champions [current law] land_ownership_law_events.5 Seeking to eliminate the need of middlemen by bringing together [ farmers getpoptype name ], Laborers, and [ shopkeepers getpoptype name ] in a co-oporatively owned business, a newly formed farmers' co-operative in [ farmer_coops_state_scope state name ]—desiring to improve the lot of their constituent members—have used their considerable local influence to push for the enactment of [current law]. Each family had come together, whether they made chairs, picked fruit, or tallied who sold what. It was agreed that the best chance of a future was to wrest control of where everything went. Fruits and furniture would stay under everyone's reach. Vegetables and vanities would be sold at a fair price, with fair splits going to who stocked the shelves. | Barn House Dealings land_ownership_law_events.7 With the government's drive to enact [current law] in full swing, a group of well-off farmers-turned-landowners from [ farmer_wealth_concentration_state_scope state name ], owing their affluence to their close proximity to the major grain markets in [ farmer_wealth_concentration_state_scope state cityhubname ], have offered to put their considerable political weight behind the law proposal, were they to be granted certain "concessions" from the government. The issue of tying land to extractive wealth is simple: whoever controls the land with valuables will have a greater voice in an amassed society. Whether this comes in the form of wheat, cotton, or opium does not matter. It invariably leads to a desire to avoid losing that greater voice. And so come the impassioned compromises: a temporary sacrifice of pride to hold on to power that bit longer. | Commercialized Agriculture Tenant Farmers | Debate | has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_building = { OR = { is_building_type = building_rye_farm is_building_type = building_wheat_farm is_building_type = building_rice_farm is_building_type = building_maize_farm is_building_type = building_millet_farm is_building_type = building_livestock_ranch } } } save_scope_as = farmer_wealth_concentration_state_scope } | I'm sure we could cough up some agricultural "subsidies". add_enactment_modifier = { name = big_farmer_corruption }add_modifier = { name = rural_financial_inducements days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small } Grant them preferential land rights in [ farmer_wealth_concentration_state_scope state name ]. add_enactment_modifier = { name = big_farmer_corruption }ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = farmland_consolidation_modifier_pos days = normal_modifier_time } } We can do without such skulduggery. | A Tragedy in the Commons land_ownership_law_events.8 With the debate about the enactment of [current law] looming large in [ a_tragedy_in_the_commons_state_scope state name ], a local dispute between a [ a_tragedy_in_the_commons_state_scope state farmhubname ] landowner and several farmers has come to bloodshed. Faced with the possibility of their common grazing lands being bought up and closed off, one particularly irate farmer responded by gunning down the landowner in a fit of rage. A death is a tragedy, and a million is a statistic. The statistics in this case being acres of land, and lives displaced by an act of greed. Killing a perpetrator is a swift fix. But the mere act of murder itself has condemned one party to hardship. | An Affront to the Garden land_ownership_law_events.11 [ majority_religion pop religion name ] clerics are handing out pamphlets declaring [ruler] and [ industrialists_leader_scope character herhis ] works on [current law] are an affront to the world's natural order. There are no true means to say which gods care on how a crop grows. Those with any true sense would place their faith in the soil they use. Fertilize good soil, care for it well, and grow only those plants that do not harm each other. And yet, those with earthly power will presume to know what their god demands when that power is threatened. | Collectivized Agriculture | Stall | ig:ig_devout ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no ig_approval < 5 }NOT = { country_has_state_religion = rel:animist } | random_scope_pop = { limit = { has_state_religion = yes } save_scope_as = majority_religion } ruler = { save_scope_as = ruler_scope } | All the more reason to press ahead. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress }every_interest_group = { limit = { OR = { leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist } leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat } is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivisation days = normal_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = clergymen }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = peasants } The process cannot continue if our [ majority_religion pop religion name ] population revolts. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress multiplier = 0.5 }random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_capitulated_to_religious_agitation days = normal_modifier_time } } Seize these pamphlets and use them for lantern fuel. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_religious_agitation_against_collectivization_progress }ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_burned_religious_pamphlets days = normal_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = clergymen } |
| Late on Arrival land_ownership_law_events.12 State planners have returned early from touring the farms converted to [current law] in [ collectivised_state state name ]. They believe the farms cannot support the heavy machinery properly. "Suppose we could manufacture redundant machine parts. Enough steel would outlast the muds. I'm sure of it." "And you can assure these field-bred peasants their parts will always be on time? Without fail? I couldn't. And I won't have the city workers' lives ruined by farm mud. We'll have to think of something smarter." | Collectivized Agriculture | Debate | ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { ig_approval > 5 } | ordered_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } order_by = state_population position = 0 check_range_bounds = no save_scope_as = collectivised_state } ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { leader = { save_scope_as = rural_folk_leader } } | Machines exist only to aid human labor. ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planners_upset days = short_modifier_time } }ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planners_overridden days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planning_kerfluffle } An ad hoc steel mill should suffice. ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planners_basically_acceptable days = short_modifier_time } }scope:collectivised_state = { start_building_construction = building_steel_mill }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_backyard_steel_progress } Consult with [ rural_folk_leader character name ] on what to do. trigger = { exists = scope:rural_folk_leader }random_list = { 70 = { ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { leader = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planning_pop_up days = long_modifier_time } } } add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_state_planning_go_ahead } } 30 = { ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { leader = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_state_planning_pop_down days = long_modifier_time } } } add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_state_planning_delay } } } |
| Rippling, Strong Aides land_ownership_law_events.13 Farmhands between harvests have organized to aid the collectivization process. The most dedicated go door-to-door, helping speed any process they can find. "Did you see these large men by the door? They smell of earth and brine, and yet they're marching through our parlor with... stationary, by the looks of it." "I did. The strapping one was singing quite the tune. And he was a union man. Doesn't let any liquor pass his lips either. He seemed over the moon that I asked him to write to me." "Good God, did... did you mention me at all, by any chance?" | Collectivized Agriculture | Advance |  |  | We welcome their support. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_collectivisation_assisted } Ensure they are paid for their efforts. add_modifier = { name = modifier_collectivisation_bonus_payments days = short_modifier_time multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_collectivisation_assisted_major } |
| Picturesque Community, Now Surreal land_ownership_law_events.14 An academic known for isolationist views has released an article condemning [current law]. The article states that it would ruin the rural idyll. "With no fields of their own to work, our working men will cease being men. They will be replaced by the groan of the machine, the racket of the engine, and the ceaseless, choking fog that permeates a factory district. A quiet life chased away by the endless march of urbanization!" | Commercialized Agriculture | Stall | NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture } |  | Stay the course. ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened days = short_modifier_time } }ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened_enaction } Time to open a fresh barrel of ink... ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 } }ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_rural_idyll_threatened_enaction multiplier = 2 } |
| Foreign Farm Acquisitions, Inc. land_ownership_law_events.15 Debates between Landowners and Industrialists have spilled into the [country-adj] news. The focal point: foreign investment in [current law]. "With extraterritoriality about, what legal guarantees do we have that [country-adj] laws affect [neighbor country-adj] citizens here?" "A fairer point would be who works on that land. It's [country-adj] land, it should be worked by [country-adj] hands!" | Total Top-Down Management land_ownership_law_events.16 The Industrialists have taken an interest in [current law]. Many industry leaders hope to secure the entire supply chain for their companies. "Gentlemen, I present to you a model of the future: [ industrialists_leader_scope character name ]landia. This diorama, sourced from pieces of this project, aims to capture the whole production chain. Cut out the middle-men, as the High Street peddlers like to say. We own the farms to feed the workers, the mines to supply our furnaces, and the cotton for our uniforms. One holistic chain, focusing on the sale and distribution of our final product. Questions?" | Commercialized Agriculture | Advance | NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_vertically_integrated_agriculture } } | ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig leader = { save_scope_as = industrialists_leader_scope } } | Middle-managers will be a thing of the past. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_total_top_down_management }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_vertically_integrated_agriculture sponsor = scope:industrialists_ig cooldown = 120 } } The workers will need additional protections. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_ttdm_demanded_workers_protections } An interesting proposal. | Family Always Comes First land_ownership_law_events.17 A court clash over [current law] erupts between a few families. The early-bird homesteaders each claim the same land in [ claimed_state state name ]. The dispute has called the legislation into question. Three claims, all issued by the same office, crossed the magistrate's desk. The details were correct: all three had legal writs to the same plot of land. Each bore a different signature. The magistrate knew the pen strokes of those clerks well. Surely there was a mistake. Though such a simple explanation wouldn't satisfy the wild looks in the family matriarchs' eyes. | Homesteading Peasant Proprietorship | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship }any_scope_state = { free_arable_land > 0 } | random_scope_state = { limit = { free_arable_land > 0 } save_scope_as = claimed_state } | Void the writs and organize reparations. add_modifier = { name = modifier_land_dispute_reparations months = 12 multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_land_dispute_tangled } Find the earliest signee and grant them the land. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_land_dispute_tangled_rurals } Divide the land between all three. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_land_dispute_tangled_landowners } |
| Peasantry, Rebranded land_ownership_law_events.18 A clash of thought has erupted in [capital state]. Rural firebrands have criticized the [current law] laws for supporting a master-servant relationship with the Landowners. "Feh. We work too much for that madame. All I'm saying is that if I wanted a woman to take all I work for, chain me to a bed, and call her mistress, I'd rather actually owe her money, first." "The nearest bordello is three towns away." "Oh, I were thinkin' of a marriage dowry." | Tenant Farmers | Stall | has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom | ig:ig_landowners ?= { save_scope_as = landowners_ig } capital = { save_scope_as = capital_scope } | Perhaps a revision is in order. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded } People will work what they are worth. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded_landlords } Concessions must not avert the Revolution. add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_peasantry_rebranded_concessions }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals strata = lower } |
| Darwin Among the Machines luddite_law_events.1 An article published in the [ GetCityHubName scope rootscope country capital cityhubname ] [newspaper] has been making headways within the country's literary circles, enflaming the ongoing debate surrounding [current law]. Claiming that machines are a kind of mechanical life undergoing constant evolution, it warns that, unless mankind acts prudently, machines might one day supplant humans as the dominant species. "We refer to the question: What sort of creature man's next successor in the supremacy of the earth is likely to be. We have often heard this debated; but it appears to us that we are ourselves creating our own successors. Day by day the machines are gaining ground upon us; day by day we are becoming more subservient to them; more men are daily bound down as slaves to tend them, more men are daily devoting the energies of their whole lives to the development of mechanical life. The upshot is simply a question of time." | Industry Banned | Advance | year >= 1859 # Origin of the Species |  | The machines shall not replace us. add_enactment_modifier = { name = machine_men_with_machine_minds_modifier } Sounds like someone has sat in his dank studio for a bit too long. add_modifier = { name = time_to_go_outside_modifier days = normal_modifier_time } Fascinating idea. Rather than renegotiate contracts, they are trying to renegotiate the laws by force. | From Many to One subjecthood_events.1 The [ GetAdjectiveNoFlag root country adjectivenoflag ] state's embrace of [ relevant_primary_culture culture name ] nationalism has raised fears of forced assimilation amongst [ relevant_minority_culture culture name ] communities. They have pledged opposition to any attempt to enact [current law]. The people of the village had long been loyal subjects of the [title]. Certainly, there had been troubles - tax evasion, individual rebellions, the typical grumbling of disaffected peasants - but they had been loyal, in the end. And then the news from [capital city] came, and a great feeling of unease accompanied it. The government would now issue its decrees in [ relevant_primary_culture culture name ], a language few of them spoke and even fewer of them read. The [ relevant_minority_culture culture name ] language would no longer be good enough. "Learn", it seemed to say. | The Ideal Citizenry subjecthood_events.2 Discussions on the nature of national citizenship have emerged in the salons of [ relevant_state state cityhubname ]. The vision of [ relevant_primary_culture culture name ] primacy under [current law] has come to conflict with more universalist ideals. "Major [ name_a_scope character name ] is conspiring, again." [ name_b_scope character name ] swivelled his head to follow his friend's thumb. There, indeed, was the Major, clad in the same uniform as ever. He was leaning in to discuss something with his companion, more for appearances than any genuine desire for secrecy. "So he is. "Honor and loyalty", he always says, "honor and loyalty". ## Agitator law events

The following events arise from having an [agitator](Agitator.md) during law enactment. They all require that an agitator either approves or disapproves of the current enacting law.

| Event | Enacting laws | Event type | Other conditions | Relevant IGs and characters | Options |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A Rant for the Ages agitator_legal_events.1 Road traffic in [capital city] has been halted as [ opposing_agitator character name ] drew a crowd into a major avenue and rambled at nauseating length about the government's proposal for [current law]. The blockage has prevented the legislature from reaching quorum. "Furthermore, in the context of the fourth paragraph pertaining to the legal definition of 'stroke,' the current legal copy does not specify as to which stroke is defined. Many may confuse the stroke of a brush or hammer for the stroke suffered by the elderly and unfortunate alike." |  | Stall | #Triggers when you enact laws; agitator does not like the law AND governing interest group doesn't care about the law agitator_opposes_enaction = yes government_ig_ambivalent_enaction = yes | #Immediately, the law you're working on resets its timer — it's stalled — and the options will target the Agitator's IG and SOL Radicals save_agitator_law_scopes = yes capital = { save_scope_as = capital_scope } | Perhaps it is time to legalize gag laws. random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes NOT = { is_same_interest_group_type = scope:opposing_agitator.interest_group } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_legalised_gag_laws days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals interest_group = scope:opposing_agitator.interest_group }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_wasted_session } Smear the talkative fool. Publicly. add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = scope:opposing_agitator.interest_group }scope:opposing_agitator = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_public_smears days = short_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_wasted_session multiplier = 2 } |

As the supervisor entered the office, eyes bleary from a restless night's sleep, he rapidly came to be aware of the pained expressions on the faces of his staff. ## Instant law events

These laws result in the instant enactment of a law, and have a much higher chance of triggering compared to generic events.[2]

| Event | Enacting laws | Event type | Other conditions | Relevant IGs and characters | Options |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| The Trial of [title] [ruler] 1848.1 The radical government has imprisoned [title] [ruler] and hastily organized a trial. The expected outcome is a guilty verdict followed by public execution. "Citizen [ GetLastNameNoFormatting root country ruler name ]. The [country-adj] nation accuses you of having committed a multitude of crimes to establish your tyranny, to destroy her freedom. Most egregious among these charges include conspiring with foreign powers, aristocratic corruption, and causing the blood of the people to flow. How do you plead?" | Parliamentary Republic Presidential Republic | Advance / Debate / Stall [3] | has_law = law_type:law_monarchy OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic }any_interest_group = { is_in_government = yes leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical } }NOT = { has_modifier = regicide_averted }ruler = { is_immortal = no } |  | Guilty! ## Meiji Restoration law events

Main article:
Japanese content#Restoration: End Sakoku
These events can only trigger when the Journal Entry [Restoration: End Sakoku](https://vic3.paradoxwikis.com/Restoration:_End_Sakoku) is active as [Japan](Japan.md), but they can also trigger outside the law enactment process as a random event.

| Event | Enacting laws | Event type | Other conditions | Relevant participants | Options |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Consultant country-adj] Military Consultants meiji.4 Taking interest in Japan 's modernization efforts, [consultant country] has offered us the service of General [consultant] as a military consultant. The terms of this arrangement appear favorable, but we should be wary of indebting ourselves to foreign powers. "This, sir, is a Gatling gun. Two hundred rounds per minute. There is nothing, neither man nor beast nor machine, that can withstand the hellish cacophony of ammunition that this device can unleash. We will teach your soldiers to operate such machines, to maintain modern military discipline, and to obey their superiors without question." | Mercantilism Agrarianism Free Trade Command Economy | Advance | Does not have Western Military Consultants Western Military Consultants Country modifier +100% Technology Spread for Military Has enacted any variant of Isolationism [4] Has an active Restoration: End Sakoku journal entry | Consultant country: Random great power or major power: Relations at least neutral Is not at war with Japan Is not owed an obligation by Japan Consultant: Random available general in [consultant country] | We welcome [consultant country] assistance. Relations towards [consultant country] deteriorates by 20 If enacting Mercantilism, Protectionism, or Free Trade: [5] Gets Ending Isolation Ending Isolation Enactment modifier +10% Enactment Success Chance |
| Embassy to [embassy country] meiji.5 As part of an ambassadorial voyage to the West, our dignitaries have met with [ambassador], a senior [embassy country-adj] politician. Interested in establishing good relations with the newly opening Japanese state, they have drafted several treaties we may wish to enter with [embassy country]. "Nations these days all appear to conduct relations with amity and courtesy. But this is entirely superficial; for behind this facade lurks mutual contempt and a struggle for supremacy. ## Tanzimat law events

Main article:
Turkish content#The Sick Man of Europe
These events can only trigger when the Journal Entry [The Sick Man of Europe](https://vic3.paradoxwikis.com/The_Sick_Man_of_Europe) is active as the [Ottoman Empire](Ottoman Empire.md).

| Event | Enacting laws | Event type | Other conditions | Relevant IGs and characters | Options |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| The Iltizām System tanzimat_events.1 For centuries our administration has depended on tax farming for collecting revenue, but this highly inefficient system leaves much to be desired. Proposals for reform have flooded the Grand Vizier's desk, and the nation eagerly awaits our response. Ever since the reign of Mehmed II, taxation rights have been auctioned off to the highest bidder, who in turn retain a part of the revenue for personal use. Over time, this has created a cadre of powerful, autonomous, and oftentimes corrupt Local Governors, significantly reducing state revenues. While most agree that the system must change, few agree on how, and fewer still want to antagonize the landholding elite. | Consumption-Based Taxation Per-Capita Taxation Proportional Taxation Graduated Taxation | Debate | The The Sick Man of Europe journal entry is progressing This event has not happened before Has enacted any variant of Land-Based Taxation |  | Abolish tax farming, but leave the Local Governors in place. AI chance: 25% Gets New System, Old Faces New System, Old Faces Country modifier +5% Bureaucracy for 20 years Gets Limited Governor Support For Reform Limited Governor Support For Reform Enactment modifier +10% Enactment Success Chance Let sleeping dogs lie. AI chance: 25% Local Governors gets Retained Taxation Rights Retained Taxation Rights Interest group modifier +3 Interest Group Approval for 10 years Gets Broad Governor Support For Reform Broad Governor Support For Reform Enactment modifier −15% Enactment Speed +10% Enactment Success Chance Abolish the system in fact as well as in name. AI chance: 50% Enactment progresses by 1 phase Gets Iltizām Abolished Iltizām Abolished Country modifier +10% Bureaucracy for 20 years Local Governors gets Rescinded Taxation Rights Rescinded Taxation Rights Interest group modifier −3 Interest Group Approval −15% Interest Group Pop Attraction for 10 years |
| An Ottoman Land Code tanzimat_events.2 Our existing system of land ownership is old, inconsistent, and in dire need of reform. Proponents of modernization demand action; how shall the High Porte respond? Land ownership in the Ottoman Empire is a complicated affair, consisting of an overlapping patchwork of tenured and communal lands, tax-exempt villages, grants of conquest, and a slew of other land categories. Recognizing the current situation's negative impact on state administration and revenue, key figures in Constantinople push for reform. Fearing conscription and increased taxation however, the Empire's rural population are sure to disapprove of any change to the status quo. | Agrarianism Interventionism Laissez-Faire Command Economy | Debate | The The Sick Man of Europe journal entry is progressing This event has not happened before Has enacted any variant of Traditionalism |  | Reform the system, but recognize traditional land categories. Gets Reformed Land Code Reformed Land Code Country modifier +5% Bureaucracy for 20 years 5% of peasants pops become more radical Some peasants might resist the reform. (The event Peasants Resist Land Registration can potentially trigger) The old system has served us well. Local Governors gets Traditional Land System Retained Traditional Land System Retained Interest group modifier +3 Interest Group Approval for 5 years Rural Folk gets Traditional Land System Retained Traditional Land System Retained Interest group modifier +3 Interest Group Approval for 5 years Enact a truly modern land code. Gets Modern Land Code Modern Land Code Country modifier +10% Bureaucracy for 20 years 10% of peasants pops become more radical Some peasants might resist the reform. (The event Peasants Resist Land Registration can potentially trigger) |
| Islâhat Hatt-ı Hümâyûnu tanzimat_events.4 With our move towards a more professional bureaucratic system in motion, demands have been put forth to nominally elevate the legal status of non-Muslims, enabling them to serve as civil servants. The preservation of the Sublime State in the face of unrest, insurrection, and foreign intervention is a, if not the, primary concern of the [Sultan]. If we are to achieve this however, steps to appease our minority subjects might be necessary, lest they become pawns of malicious foreign powers. | Appointed Bureaucrats Elected Bureaucrats | Debate | The The Sick Man of Europe journal entry is progressing This event has not happened before Any pop in Ottoman Empire is Any pop in Ottoman Empire is a Christian ( / / / ) religion Has enacted any variant of Hereditary Bureaucrats |  | Equal rights under the law! On paper at least. The Empire's non-Muslim subjects have traditionally been exempt from military service, substituting their blood with a special 'cizye' tax paid to the High Porte.
